227:	def bisection(func, low, high, eps):	if low > high:	low, high = high, low	flow = func(low)	fhigh = func(high)	if flow*fhigh > 0:	print("root is not bracketed by [", low, ", ", high,"]")	exit(1)	mid = 0.5*(high+low)	fmid = func(mid)	while (high-low) > eps and math.abs(fmid) > eps:	if fmid*flow < 0:	high = mid	fhigh = fmid	else:	low = mid	flow = fmid	mid = 0.5*(high+low)	fmid = func(mid)	return mid

262:	def sortedSquares(nums):	res = [0] * len(nums)	l, r, i = 0, len(nums) - 1, len(nums) - 1	while l <= r:	if nums[l] * nums[l] < nums[r] * nums[r]:	res[i] = nums[l] * nums[l]	l, i = l + 1, i - 1	else:	res[i] = nums[r] * nums[r]	r, i = r - 1, i - 1	return res

292:	def multinv(modulus, value):	if modulus <= 1:	return None	lastx, x = 1, 0	a, b = modulus, value	while b != 0:	q = a // b	temp = b	b, a = a % b, temp	temp = x	x, lastx = lastx - q * x, x	result = (1 - lastx * modulus) // value	if result < 0:	result = result + modulus	if result >= modulus:	result = result % modulus	if value * result % modulus == 1:	return result	return None

962:	def covariance_matrix(nums_with_uncert):	n = len(nums_with_uncert)	matrix = [[0 for _ in range(n)] for _ in range(n)]	for i in range(n):	for j in range(n):	matrix[i][j] = 0	for i, expr1 in enumerate(nums_with_uncert):	vars1 = set(expr1.derivatives)	coefs_expr1 = [0 for _ in range(n)]	for j, expr2 in enumerate(nums_with_uncert):	vars2 = set(expr2.derivatives)	coef = 0	for var in vars1.intersection(vars2):	coef += (expr1.derivatives[var]*expr2.derivatives[var]*var._std_dev**2)	coefs_expr1[j] = coef	for j, coef in enumerate(coefs_expr1):	matrix[i][j] = coef	matrix[j][i] = coef

1883:	def longest_increasing_subsequence(X):	N = len(X)	P = [0] * N	M = [0] * (N + 1)	L = 0	for i in range(1, N + 1):	lo = 1	hi = L	while lo <= hi:	mid = (lo + hi) // 2	if X[M[mid]] < X[i - 1]:	lo = mid + 1	else:	hi = mid - 1	new_l = lo	P[i] = M[new_l - 1]	M[new_l] = i	if new_l > L:	L = new_l	S = [0] * L	k = M[L]	for i in range(L - 1, -1, -1):	S[i] = X[k - 1]	k = P[k]	return S

2067:	def bisect(f, lo, hi, eps):	assert(f(lo) != f(hi))	if (hi is None):	hi = lo + 1	while (f(hi) == f(lo)):	hi = hi * 2	hi = hi - 1	else:	assert(f(hi) != f(lo))	while (hi - lo > eps):	mid = (hi + lo) / 2	if (f(mid) == f(lo)):	lo = mid	else:	hi = mid	return lo

2802:	def solve_theta(theta, gamma, gainratio):	theta_k1 = theta	eps = 1e-6 * theta	drv, phi, dphi = 0, 0, 0	while abs(phi) > eps:	drv = gamma * pow(theta_k1, gamma-1) + gainratio	theta_k1 = theta_k1 - phi / drv	phi = pow(theta_k1, gamma) - gainratio * (1-theta_k1)	dphi = gamma * pow(theta_k1, gamma-2)	return theta_k1

2944:	def bb_intersection_over_union(box_a, box_b):	x_a = max(box_a[1], box_b[1])	y_a = max(box_a[2], box_b[2])	x_b = min(box_a[3], box_b[3])	y_b = min(box_a[4], box_b[4])	intersect_area = max(0, x_b - x_a + 1) * max(0, y_b - y_a + 1)	box_a_area = (box_a[3] - box_a[1] + 1) * (box_a[4] - box_a[2] + 1)	box_b_area = (box_b[3] - box_b[1] + 1) * (box_b[4] - box_b[2] + 1)	iou = intersect_area / (box_a_area + box_b_area - intersect_area)	return iou

3132:	def _Bez3step(b, r, alpha):	if alpha == 0.0:	return [b[1], b[2], b[3], b[4]]	elif alpha == 1.0:	return [b[r], b[r+1], b[r+2], b[r+3]]	else:	beta = 1.0 - alpha	cp_left = {}	cp_right = {}	for i in range(1, 2 * r + 1):	cp_left[i] = b[i]	cp_right[i] = b[i + 4 - r]	lp = _Bez1step(cp_left, r, alpha)	rp = _Bez1step(cp_right, r, beta)	ans = {}	for i in range(1, 4 - r):	ans[i] = {}	for j in range(1, len(b[1])):	ans[i][j] = lp[i] * beta + rp[i] * alpha	return ans

3317:	def td_lambda(rewards, n_step, gamma):	eligibilities = {}	for i in range(len(rewards)):	eligibility = {}	eligibilities[i] = eligibility	for t in range(i, i+n_step):	if t not in eligibilities:	eligibilities[t] = {}	if t == i:	for k in range(len(eligibilities[t])):	eligibilities[t][k] = 0.0	if rewards[t] is not None:	for k in range(len(eligibilities[t])):	eligibilities[t][k] = gamma * eligibilities[t][k] + 1.0	returns = []	for i in range(len(rewards)):	r = 0.0	for t in range(i, i+n_step):	if rewards[t] is not None:	r = r + rewards[t]	returns.append(r)	return returns

3880:	def calculateIoU(startX_, startY_, endX_, endY_, x1, y1, x2, y2):	x_left = max(startX_, x1)	y_top = max(startY_, y1)	x_right = min(endX_, x2)	y_bottom = min(endY_, y2)	if x_right < x_left or y_bottom < y_top:	return 0.0	intersection_area = (x_right - x_left) * (y_bottom - y_top)	bb1_area = (endX_ - startX_) * (endY_ - startY_)	bb2_area = (x2 - x1) * (y2 - y1)	iou = intersection_area / (bb1_area + bb2_area - intersection_area)	return iou

4076:	def regular_falsi(f, interval):	a, b = interval	def f_a(x):	return f(x, a)	def f_b(x):	return f(x, b)	def f_ab(x):	return f(x, a, b)	c = a - f_a(a) * (b - a) / (f_b(b) - f_a(a))	c_f_c = f_c(c)	c_f_ab = f_c(c, a, b)	if c_f_c * c_f_ab > 0:	return None	return [(a, c), (c, b)]
